Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The synthesis variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 965 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 583 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_965(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 965. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^583(S, F) is trivial. QED.
